2026-08-24: PRBS/TDC test investigation + TDC test built.
- TDC test: DONE. cfgRunTdcTest() in SciFi_Configuration.js, a "TDC
  test" button per MuTrig row. Injects TDC test pulses (tdctest_n=false
  on the MuTrig's 32 channels, active-low) + SciFiFibre/Commands/
  TestPulsesTDC=true, waits 1.5s, reads FCCH (hit rate) back, shows
  PASS/FAIL, always restores tdctest_n/TestPulsesTDC to normal after.
  Built from real, already-live production pieces (same tdctest_n/
  TestPulsesTDC combo mhttpd/custom/Scifi-monitor.js's configure_pll()
  already uses as a PLL-test prerequisite) - not invented from scratch.
  PASS threshold (hits > 0 Hz) is explicitly flagged as provisional/
  untuned, shown in both the button tooltip and the result message.
  Result shown as a statusPill (same look as ScanThr/PllTest pills)
  next to the button - Running/PASS/FAIL/Aborted, in-memory only
  (cfgTdcTestResults Map, resets on page reload, same as
  cfgSelectedChannels). "Never run" state renders nothing (no pill) at
  the user's request, instead of a permanent neutral pill.
  User asked "is TDC global like PRBS too?" - checked
  MutrigFEB::ChangeTDCTest: NOT fully MuTrig-isolated like the button
  wording first implied - TestPulsesTDC's handler loops over and flips
  a counter-control bit on EVERY enabled FEB, not just the target one.
  Only tdctest_n (real per-channel array, correctly written for just
  the channels being tested) keeps the actual pulse RESPONSE scoped -
  so the trigger is global-ish but the effect is channel-scoped, a
  hybrid, unlike PRBS/tx_mode which has no per-channel scoping at all.
  Button tooltip + code comment corrected to say this accurately.
  Restructured to 3 trigger tiers, same shape as the existing PE-band
  picker (cfgPeLevelSelect/ForFeb + cfg-bulk-pe-select): cfgRunTdcTest
  (single MuTrig, the original row button) / cfgRunTdcTestForFeb (new -
  "TDC test" button on the FEB row, tests every MuTrig in that
  Super-module at once) / cfgRunTdcTestForSelected (new - "TDC test
  (selected)" button in the top calib-actions toolbar, uses the same
  cfgResolveSelection() the other bulk buttons already use). All three
  now call one shared cfgRunTdcTestOnScope(pairs) taking a {feb,asic}[]
  list, same "one function, N callers" pattern as cfgApplyMaskToScope/
  cfgApplyEnableToScope.
- PRBS test: CLOSED, same day - user explicitly accepted the tx_mode=1/0
  Enable/Disable pair as sufficient, no longer waiting on Marius/Yfeng
  before considering this item done. The MuTRiG3 bit-combination table
  for tx_mode values beyond 0/1 remains unconfirmed and out of scope
  unless priorities change later - not a blocker anymore, just a
  known limitation of what's exposed.
- PRBS test: PARTIALLY UNBLOCKED, same day. User's own point: tx_mode=1
  is already the exact value used in production by Scifi-monitor.js's
  configure_prbs() - no need to wait for the full MuTRiG3 bit-
  combination table just to make basic PRBS start/stop possible, only
  to expose modes beyond that one confirmed value. Added "Enable PRBS"/
  "Disable PRBS" buttons (cfgSetTxMode(1)/cfgSetTxMode(0)) in
  Configuration's "SwitchingFibre commands" toolbar, new "PRBS (global)"
  group - writes CFG_GLOBAL_TXMODE_PATH then triggers SciFiFibre/
  Commands/MutrigConfig (same push-to-hardware step configure_prbs()
  uses), plus a raw tx_mode readback. Deliberately does NOT expose any
  other tx_mode value (2/3/4/...) - those remain unconfirmed for
  MuTRiG3. No automated verdict cycle built (unlike TDC test) - PASS/
  FAIL for PRBS still means watching FCCE (CRC errors, already shown
  per MuTrig) manually after enabling; automating that is a possible
  follow-up, not done now.
- Original investigation notes (still accurate): found
  tx_mode's real bit-mapping for MuTRiG2 in Mutrig2Config.cpp (0=normal,
  1=PRBS single, 2=PRBS debug, 3=short-event mode; path /Equipment/
  SciFiFibre/Settings/ASICs/Global/tx_mode, already written in
  production by Scifi-monitor.js's configure_prbs() with tx_mode=1).
  The real system uses MuTRiG3 (is_mutrig_3=1 in local ODB) though, and
  Mutrig3Config.cpp only says tx_mode "combine flags to get more
  options" (3 combinable bits) with no bit-position table found in the
  source checked - genuinely still needs Marius/Yfeng to confirm the
  MuTRiG3 encoding before writing any tx_mode value on a real MuTrig3
  FEB. Confirmed via odbedit that tx_mode really is a single scalar
  (#Val 1, not an array) - it's a GLOBAL flag for the whole SciFi
  detector, not per-FEB or per-MuTrig (user caught this - my own
  earlier framing implied a per-MuTrig button, wrong scope). Real
  local ODB value right now is 4, outside MuTRiG2's 0-3 range - further
  evidence MuTRiG3's encoding genuinely differs, not just unconfirmed.
  Placement once unblocked: goes in Configuration's existing
  "SwitchingFibre commands" toolbar (same group as Bypass/Running-Idle/
  Reset Counters), NOT a per-MuTrig row button like TDC test got.
  Planned verdict mechanism once unblocked: same shape as TDC test,
  read FCCE (CRC errors) back - PRBS pattern correct on the receive side
  should keep CRC errors at 0.

2026-09-02: Marius (live, Zoom) asked two questions about the new page
that surfaced a real functional gap, confirmed against the firmware
source (switching_pc/slowcontrol/mutrig/Mutrig_FEB.cpp):
1. Channels/mask, tthresh, tthresh_offset etc. are NOT hotlinked in the
   frontend (unlike dummy_config/reset_*/lapse_*, which apply live on
   ODB change) - they only reach the chips via ConfigureASICs(),
   triggered by the MutrigConfig command. On the page, MutrigConfig was
   only ever fired as a side effect of Enable/Disable PRBS
   (cfgSetTxMode) - Mask/Unmask/Enable/Disable asic buttons
   (cfgApplyMaskToScope/cfgApplyEnableToScope) wrote ODB only and never
   applied to hardware. FIXED: added a standalone "Push config
   (MutrigConfig)" button (cfg-configure-btn / cfgTriggerMutrigConfig)
   in the SwitchingFibre commands toolbar, triggers MutrigConfig alone.
2. Marius: "special config to set the chip into PLL mode, like PRBS,
   present on the old page" - confirmed real, NOT built on the new
   page. Old Scifi-monitor.js's configure_pll() sets tx_mode=4 PLUS a
   batch of channel-level settings (cml, cml_sc, tdctest_n, recv_all,
   dmon_select, mask) then a TestPulsesTDC+MutrigConfig sequence - not
   just a tx_mode flip like PRBS. This conflicts with the 2026-08-28 PLL
   Test redesign's assumption ("questo e gia implementato nel firmware")
   that PLL-mode entry was firmware-automatic - that assumption covered
   the DAC-sweep loop, may not have covered chip-mode entry itself.
   NOT built (needs Marius/Yfeng to confirm whether it's still required
   and what MuTRiG3's real tx_mode=4-equivalent + channel settings are,
   same external-dependency shape as the PRBS MuTRiG3 bit table above -
   left open, flagged back to the user, not guessed at).

BUILT 2026-09-02: Marius confirmed live ("should be in the javascript
of the old page") - configure_pll()'s values ARE the answer, ported.
Correcting my own earlier framing: tx_mode=4 is NOT a PLL-specific
value - Scifi-monitor.js's configure_short()/0pe5/1pe5/2pe5 (the
NORMAL physics-mode configs) also write tx_mode=4. What actually
differs between "PLL mode" and "normal" is cml/cml_sc/tdctest_n/mask,
not tx_mode - so tx_mode=4 is the already-production-proven baseline
value, not an untested bit. Added two buttons, "Configure PLL
(selected)" / "Revert to normal (selected)", scoped to the current
FEB/MuTrig selection (cfgResolveSelection, same as TDC test/PE bulk):
- Configure: tx_mode=4 (global, whole detector - can't be scoped,
  labelled as such), then per-channel in scope mask=1/cml=0/cml_sc=1/
  tdctest_n=0/recv_all=1, per-asic in scope dmon_select=-1, then
  TestPulsesTDC false->true, then MutrigConfig. All field names/paths
  verified live against the local ODB (Channels/mask,cml,cml_sc,
  tdctest_n,recv_all all exist at 3072 width; TDCs/dmon_select exists
  at 96 width - dmon_select was NOT renamed to dmon_sel as I'd
  worried, both fields actually exist, dmon_select is the right one).
- Revert: per-channel mask=0/cml=8/cml_sc=0/tdctest_n=1/recv_all=1
  (configure_short()'s own normal-mode values), TestPulsesTDC off,
  MutrigConfig. tx_mode deliberately left untouched (see above - it's
  shared with normal mode too, nothing to revert).
Deliberately NOT ported (flagged, not silently dropped): the old
code's `for (asic=8; asic<16) mask[asic*32+4]=0` loop (unmasks one
specific channel per 8 specific ASICs - looks like a hand-tuned
historical test setup, doesn't generalize to "any selected ASIC");
and the second `Settings/ASICs/cml[*]` 8->0->configure dance right
after ("ASICs/cml" as a bare path doesn't exist in the current ODB at
all - only ASICs/{Global,TDCs,Channels} - so this reference is stale
against today's schema, not portable as written). Not tested against
real hardware, same caveat as PLL Test itself.

2026-09-02, same call, right after: the 2026-08-28 PLL Test "MAJOR
CORRECTION" (firmware drives the DAC sweep on its own) was WRONG -
Marius confirmed live the page has to drive the whole loop itself
(mask -> SMB_control/asic -> zero+set DACs -> running -> wait 1s ->
idle -> read_hist). Rebuilt `cfgPllRunStep()` accordingly, DAC inputs
editable again. Full detail in pll_test_redesign.txt's new "MAJOR
REVERSAL, 2026-09-02" section - not repeated here.
